2800async function appLoad(){
2801  if (DB && USER){
2802    try { UID = await USER.id(); } catch(e){ UID = null; }
2803  }
2804  if (DB && UID){
2805    ACCT_REF = DB.doc('data/users/' + UID + '/account');
2806    try {
2807      const snap = await ACCT_REF.get();
2808      if (snap.exists) S.acct = Object.assign(S.acct, snap.data(), { comms: Object.assign({}, S.acct.comms, (snap.data()||{}).comms) });
2809      PERSISTED = true;
2810    } catch(e){ PERSISTED = false; }
2811    if (PERSISTED){
2812      try { S.profiles  = (await sub('profiles').get()).docs.map(d => Object.assign({id:d.id}, d.data())); } catch(e){}
2813      try { S.bookings  = (await sub('bookings').orderBy('date','desc').limit(100).get()).docs.map(d => Object.assign({id:d.id}, d.data())); } catch(e){}
2814      try { S.messages  = (await sub('messages').orderBy('at','desc').limit(100).get()).docs.map(d => Object.assign({id:d.id}, d.data())); } catch(e){}
2815    }
2816  }
2817  if (!PERSISTED){
2818    if (MEM.acct) S.acct = MEM.acct;
2819    S.profiles = MEM.profiles; S.bookings = MEM.bookings; S.messages = MEM.messages;
2820  }
2821  if (!S.profiles.length){
2822    const p = { id:'p'+uid4(), name:'My home', contact:'', email:'', phone:'', type:'Household',
2823                line1:'', suburb:'', postcode:'', state:'QLD', access:'', care:'', del:'', svc:'wf',
2824                eco:false, preferred:true, createdAt:Date.now() };
2825    S.profiles.push(p); S.acct.activeId = p.id;
2826    if (!PERSISTED) MEM.profiles = S.profiles;
2827  }
2828  if (!S.acct.activeId) S.acct.activeId = S.profiles[0].id;
2829  APP_READY = true;
2830}
2831
2832async function saveAcct(){
2833  if (!PERSISTED){ MEM.acct = S.acct; return true; }
2834  try { await ACCT_REF.set(S.acct); return true; } catch(e){ console.error('[LK]', e); return false; }
2835}
2836async function saveProfile(p){
2837  if (!PERSISTED){ MEM.profiles = S.profiles; return true; }
2838  try { const {id, ...rest} = p; await sub('profiles').doc(id).set(rest); return true; }
2839  catch(e){ console.error('[LK]', e); return false; }
2840}
2841async function pushDoc(name, rec){
2842  if (!PERSISTED){ MEM[name] = S[name]; return true; }
2843  try { await sub(name).doc(rec.id).set((({id,...r})=>r)(rec)); return true; }
2844  catch(e){ console.error('[LK]', e); return false; }
2845}
2846function addMessage(ref, title, body){
2847  const m = { id:'m'+uid4(), ref, title, at:Date.now(), unread:true,
2848              turns:[{ who:'system', text:body, at:Date.now() }] };
2849  S.messages.unshift(m); pushDoc('messages', m); return m;
2850}

2852/* ---- pricing estimate ---- */
2853function estimate(svc, a, b){
2854  if (svc === 'comm') return null;
2855  let lines = [], subtot = 0;
2856  if (svc === 'wf'   ){ const v=a*PX.load; subtot+=v; lines.push([a+' × wash & fold load'+(a>1?'s':''), v]); }
2857  if (svc === 'wfi'  ){ const w=a*PX.load, i=b*PX.iron; subtot+=w+i;
2858                        lines.push([a+' × wash & fold load'+(a>1?'s':''), w]);
2859                        if (b) lines.push([b+' × item'+(b>1?'s':'')+' ironed', i]); }
2860  if (svc === 'iron' ){ const v=a*PX.iron; subtot+=v; lines.push([a+' × item'+(a>1?'s':'')+' ironed', v]); }
2861  if (svc === 'dc'   ){ const v=a*PX.dcDress; subtot+=v; lines.push([a+' × garment'+(a>1?'s':'')+' (indicative average)', v]); }
2862  const p = activeP();
2863  if (p && p.eco){ const v = PX.ecoAddon; subtot += v; lines.push(['Fragrance-free / sensitive detergent', v]); }
2864  lines.push(['Collection & return', PX.delivery]); subtot += PX.delivery;
2865  const total = Math.max(PX.minimum, subtot);
2866  if (total > subtot + 0.001) lines.push(['Minimum order adjustment', total - subtot]);
2867  return { lines, total };
2868}
2869const sumRows = est => est.lines.map(([l,v]) => '<div class="sumr"><span>'+esc(l)+'</span><span>'+money(v)+'</span></div>').join('')
2870  + '<div class="sumr tot"><span>Estimated total</span><span>'+money(est.total)+'</span></div>';
2871

2875function renderTabs(id){
2876  const on = APP_ROUTES.includes(id);
2877  document.getElementById('tabs').classList.toggle('live', on);
2878  document.body.style.paddingBottom = on ? '0' : '';
2879  document.querySelectorAll('.tab').forEach(t => {
2880    const k = t.dataset.tab;
2881    t.classList.toggle('on', k === id || (k==='messages' && id==='thread') || (k==='account' && ['profile','pdetails','prefs','address','comms','invoices','userdet','feedback','help'].includes(id)));
2882  });
2883  const n = S.messages.filter(m => m.unread).length;
2884  const b = document.getElementById('tabBadge');
2885  b.style.display = n ? '' : 'none'; b.textContent = n;
2886}

2893  document.getElementById('apHi').firstChild.textContent = nm ? greet + ' ' + nm + '!' : greet + '!';
2894  const up = S.bookings.filter(b => b.status === 'requested' || b.status === 'confirmed');
2895  document.getElementById('apHiSub').textContent = up.length
2896    ? 'You have ' + up.length + ' collection' + (up.length>1?'s':'') + ' coming up'
2897    : "Let's get your first collection booked";
2898  document.getElementById('apFutT').textContent = 'UPCOMING COLLECTIONS (' + up.length + ')';
2899  document.getElementById('apNext').innerHTML = up.length
2900    ? up.slice(0,3).map(bookingCard).join('')
2901    : '<div class="ap-card"><div class="ap-card-b ap-empty"><i>🗓</i><h3>No collections scheduled</h3>'
2902      + '<p>Book ahead to secure your preferred collection window.</p>'
2903      + '<a href="#/book" class="btn btn-red btn-lg">Book a collection</a></div></div>';
2904  [['apSwName',1],['apSwName2',1]].forEach(([id]) => { const e = document.getElementById(id); if (e) e.textContent = p ? p.name : 'My profile'; });
2905}

2907function bookingCard(b){
2908  const d = fromIso(b.date);
2909  const pill = b.status === 'cancelled' ? '<span class="pill cx">CANCELLED</span>'
2910             : b.status === 'complete'  ? '<span class="pill ok">COMPLETE</span>'
2911             : b.status === 'confirmed' ? '<span class="pill up">CONFIRMED</span>'
2912             : '<span class="pill pd">AWAITING OPERATOR</span>';
2913  return '<div class="ap-card"><div class="ap-card-h"><i>🧺</i>'+esc(SVCNAME(b.svc))+'<span style="margin-left:auto">'+pill+'</span></div>'
2914   + '<div class="ap-card-b"><div class="opt" style="margin:0;border:none;padding:0;background:none">'
2915   + '<div class="opt-d"><s>'+dayName(d)+'</s><b>'+d.getDate()+'</b><s>'+monName(d)+'</s></div>'
2916   + '<div class="opt-m"><strong>'+esc(b.window)+'</strong><small>'+esc(b.ref)+(b.recurring?' · repeats '+esc(b.recurring):'')+'</small></div>'
2917   + '<div class="opt-p">'+(b.estimate ? money(b.estimate) : '—')+'</div></div>'
2918   + (b.status==='requested'||b.status==='confirmed'
2919      ? '<button class="btn btn-out btn-full" style="margin-top:.9rem" data-cancel="'+b.id+'">Cancel this collection</button>' : '')
2920   + '</div></div>';
2921}

2923function renderBookings(){
2924  const up = S.bookings.filter(b => ['requested','confirmed'].includes(b.status));
2925  const past = S.bookings.filter(b => !['requested','confirmed'].includes(b.status));
2926  let h = '';
2927  h += '<div class="ap-sec-t">UPCOMING ('+up.length+')</div>';
2928  h += up.length ? up.map(bookingCard).join('')
2929    : '<div class="ap-card"><div class="ap-card-b ap-empty"><i>🗓</i><h3>Nothing booked yet</h3><p>Your upcoming collections will appear here.</p></div></div>';
2930  if (past.length){ h += '<div class="ap-sec-t">PAST &amp; CANCELLED ('+past.length+')</div>' + past.map(bookingCard).join(''); }
2931  document.getElementById('bkList').innerHTML = h;
2932  document.querySelectorAll('[data-cancel]').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => cancelBooking(b.dataset.cancel));
2933}

2935async function cancelBooking(id){
2936  const b = S.bookings.find(x => x.id === id); if (!b) return;
2937  b.status = 'cancelled'; b.cancelledAt = Date.now();
2938  await pushDoc('bookings', b);
2939  addMessage(b.ref, b.ref + ' has been cancelled',
2940    'Your collection ' + b.ref + ' on ' + longDate(b.date) + ', ' + b.window + ', has been cancelled. No charge applies. You can rebook any time from the Book tab.');
2941  renderBookings(); renderMessages(); renderTabs('bookings');
2942}

2963function renderThread(){

2968  document.getElementById('thrT').textContent = m.title;
2969  document.getElementById('thrS').textContent = m.ref ? 'Booking ' + m.ref : '';
2970  document.getElementById('thrBody').innerHTML = m.turns.map(t =>
2971    '<div class="bub '+(t.who==='you'?'you':'them')+'">'+esc(t.text)
2972    + '<time>'+new Date(t.at).toLocaleString('en-AU')+'</time></div>').join('');
2973  document.getElementById('thrGo').onclick = async () => {
2974    const tx = document.getElementById('thrTx').value.trim();
2975    if (!tx){ say('thrMsg', false, 'Write a message first.'); return; }
2976    m.turns.push({ who:'you', text:tx, at:Date.now() }); m.at = Date.now();
2977    await pushDoc('messages', m);
2978    document.getElementById('thrTx').value = '';
2979    await save('contact', { name:(S.acct.first+' '+S.acct.last).trim() || 'App user', email:S.acct.email,
2980      suburb:(activeP()||{}).suburb || '', topic:'Booking message', message:'['+(m.ref||'no ref')+'] '+tx },
2981      'thrMsg', 'Reply sent. We aim to respond the same business day.');
2982    renderThread();
2983  };
2984}

3010/* ---- form hydration ---- */
3011function renderForms(){
3012  const p = activeP(); if (!p) return;
3013  const set = (id, v) => { const e = document.getElementById(id); if (e) e.value = v == null ? '' : v; };
3014  const chk = (id, v) => { const e = document.getElementById(id); if (e) e.checked = !!v; };
3015  set('pdName',p.name); set('pdContact',p.contact); set('pdEmail',p.email); set('pdPhone',p.phone);
3016  set('pdType',p.type); chk('pdPref',p.preferred);
3017  set('pfCare',p.care); set('pfDel',p.del); set('pfSvc',p.svc); chk('pfEco',p.eco);
3018  set('adLine1',p.line1); set('adSub',p.suburb); set('adPc',p.postcode); set('adState',p.state); set('adAccess',p.access);
3019  set('udFirst',S.acct.first); set('udLast',S.acct.last); set('udEmail',S.acct.email); set('udPhone',S.acct.phone);
3020  const eco = document.getElementById('pfEcoPx'); if (eco) eco.textContent = ' (' + money(PX.ecoAddon) + ' per collection)';
3021  document.getElementById('cmList').innerHTML = COMMS.map(([k,t,d,locked]) =>
3022    '<div class="tgl"'+(k===COMMS[0][0]?' style="border-top:none"':'')+'>'
3023    + '<div class="tgl-t">'+t+'<small>'+d+'</small></div>'
3024    + '<button class="sw'+((locked || S.acct.comms[k]) ? ' on':'')+'"'+(locked?' disabled style="opacity:.55;cursor:not-allowed"':' data-cm="'+k+'"')+'></button></div>').join('');
3025  document.querySelectorAll('[data-cm]').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => b.classList.toggle('on'));
3026  renderInvoices();
3027}

3029function renderInvoices(){
3030  const done = S.bookings.filter(b => b.status === 'complete' && b.invoice);
3031  document.getElementById('inList').innerHTML = done.length
3032    ? done.map(b => '<div class="ap-row"><i>🧾</i><div><strong>'+esc(b.invoice)+'</strong>'
3033        + '<small>'+longDate(b.date)+' · '+esc(SVCNAME(b.svc))+'</small></div>'
3034        + '<span class="opt-p">'+money(b.charged || b.estimate)+'</span></div>').join('')
3035    : '<div class="ap-card"><div class="ap-card-b ap-empty"><i>🧾</i><h3>No invoices yet</h3>'
3036      + '<p>An invoice is issued after each collection is weighed and completed. Paid invoices stay here for your records.</p></div></div>';
3037}

3044function wizStep(n){

3047  [1,2,3].forEach(i => document.getElementById('pg'+i).classList.toggle('on', i<=Math.min(n,3)));

3049              2:['Service availability','Select your preferred collection window.'],
3050              3:['Review booking details','Add care instructions and confirm.'],
3051              4:['Booking requested','']}[n];

3053  document.getElementById('bkSub').textContent = t[1];
3054  document.getElementById('bkBack').style.visibility = n>1 && n<4 ? '' : 'hidden';
3055  window.scrollTo(0,0);
3056}

3085/* ---- helpers ---- */
3086function gcalUrl(b){
3087  if (!b.date||!b.window) return '';
3088  const d = b.date.replace(/-/g,'');
3089  const wm = { '9am':['090000','120000'], '12pm':['120000','150000'], '4pm':['160000','190000'] };
3090  const key = Object.keys(wm).find(k => b.window.startsWith(k));
3091  const [st,en] = wm[key]||['090000','120000'];
3092  const t = encodeURIComponent(`LaundryKings — ${b.service||'Collection'} — ${b.contact||b.profile||''}`);
3093  const l = encodeURIComponent(b.address||b.suburb||'');
3094  const dt = encodeURIComponent(`Ref: ${b.ref}\nCustomer: ${b.contact||b.profile||''}\nPhone: ${b.phone||''}\nCare: ${b.care||'Standard'}\nDelivery: ${b.delivery||'Safe place'}`);
3095  return `https://calendar.google.com/calendar/render?action=TEMPLATE&text=${t}&dates=${d}T${st}/${d}T${en}&location=${l}&details=${dt}`;
3096}
3097function mailtoConfirm(b, extra=''){
3098  const subj = encodeURIComponent(`LaundryKings — Collection confirmed · ${b.ref||''}`);
3099  const bod  = encodeURIComponent(
3100    `Hi ${b.contact||'there'},\n\nYour LaundryKings collection is confirmed!\n\n`
3101    +`Reference: ${b.ref||''}\nService: ${b.service||''}\nDate: ${b.date||''} · ${b.window||''}\nAddress: ${b.address||b.suburb||''}\n`
3102    +(b.operatorName?`Operator: ${b.operatorName}\n`:'')
3103    +`\nPlease have your laundry bagged and ready at the door before the start of your window.\n`
3104    +`Your operator will weigh and count everything, then confirm the final price before starting.\n`
3105    +(extra?`\n${extra}\n`:'')
3106    +`\nQuestions? Reply to this email or message us through the app.\n\nThank you!\nThe LaundryKings Team`);
3107  return `mailto:${encodeURIComponent(b.email||'')}?subject=${subj}&body=${bod}`;
3108}

3110/* ---- ops data load ---- */
3111async function opsLoad(){
3112  if (!DB||!CAN_EDIT) return;
3113  try { OPS.jobs = (await DB.collection('bookings_inbox').orderBy('createdAt','desc').limit(300).get()).docs.map(d=>Object.assign({id:d.id},d.data())); } catch(e){ OPS.jobs=[]; }
3114  try { OPS.operators = (await DB.collection('operators').get()).docs.map(d=>Object.assign({id:d.id},d.data())).filter(o=>o.active!==false); } catch(e){ OPS.operators=[]; }
3115  try {
3116    const pc = await DB.doc('config/payment').get();
3117    if (pc.exists){
3118      const pd = pc.data();
3119      if (pd.payid) document.getElementById('invPayId').textContent = `PayID: ${pd.payid}${pd.bsb?' · BSB: '+pd.bsb+' Acct: '+pd.account:''}`;
3120      if (pd.stripeLink){ const a=document.getElementById('invStripe'); a.innerHTML = `Stripe payment link: <a href="${esc(pd.stripeLink)}" target="_blank" style="color:var(--navy);text-decoration:underline">${esc(pd.stripeLink)}</a>`; }
3121    }
3122  } catch(e){}
3123}
3124async function saveJob(job){
3125  if (!DB||!CAN_EDIT) return false;
3126  const {id,...rest}=job; try{ await DB.collection('bookings_inbox').doc(id).set(rest); return true; }catch(e){ console.error('[LK ops]',e); return false; }
3127}

3159function opsCard(b){

3161  const pill = `<span class="pill ${({requested:'pd',assigned:'up',collected:'pd',invoiced:'ok',complete:'ok',cancelled:'cx'}[b.status]||'pd')}">${OPS_LABELS[b.status]||b.status}</span>`;
3162  const cal = gcalUrl(b);
3163  const opEl = b.operatorName
3164    ? `<div class="bkc-op"><div class="bkc-av">${esc(initials(b.operatorName))}</div>${esc(b.operatorName)}</div>`
3165    : OPS.operators.length
3166      ? `<div class="selw" style="min-width:150px"><select class="sel" style="font-size:.76rem;padding:.34rem .6rem" data-opassign="${b.id}"><option value="">Assign operator…</option>${OPS.operators.map(o=>`<option value="${o.id}">${esc(o.first+' '+o.last)}</option>`).join('')}</select></div>`
3167      : `<a href="#/opsops" style="font-size:.75rem;color:var(--navy);text-decoration:underline">Add operators →</a>`;
3168
3169  const acts = [];
3170  if (b.status==='requested')  acts.push(['btn-red','confirm|'+b.id,'✓ Confirm']);
3171  if (b.status==='assigned')   acts.push(['btn-nv','collect|'+b.id,'Mark collected']);
3172  if (b.status==='collected')  acts.push(['btn-red','invoice|'+b.id,'🧾 Invoice']);
3173  if (b.status==='invoiced')   acts.push(['btn-ok','paid|'+b.id,'✅ Mark paid']);
3174  acts.push(['btn-out','detail|'+b.id,'Detail ›']);
3175
3176  return `<div class="ops-c ${stCls}">
3177    <div class="ops-ch"><div><div class="ops-ref">${esc(b.ref||b.id)}</div><div class="ops-sub">${esc(b.service||'')}${b.recurring?' · recurring':''}${b.estimate?' · est. '+money(b.estimate):''}</div></div>${pill}</div>
3178    <div class="ops-b">
3179      <div class="ops-ln"><i>👤</i><span><strong>${esc(b.contact||b.profile||'Customer')}</strong>${b.email?` · ${esc(b.email)}`:''}${b.phone?` · ${esc(b.phone)}`:''}</span></div>
3180      <div class="ops-ln"><i>📅</i><span>${esc(b.date||'')} · ${esc(b.window||'')}${cal?' · <a href="'+cal+'" target="_blank">+ Calendar</a>':''}</span></div>
3181      <div class="ops-ln"><i>📍</i><span>${esc(b.address||b.suburb||'')}</span></div>
3182      ${b.care?`<div class="ops-ln"><i>👕</i><span style="color:var(--mute)">${esc(b.care)}</span></div>`:''}
3183      ${b.delivery?`<div class="ops-ln"><i>🚚</i><span style="color:var(--mute)">${esc(b.delivery)}</span></div>`:''}

3185    </div>
3186    <div class="ops-acts2">${acts.map(([cls,act,lbl])=>`<button class="btn ${cls}" data-opact="${act}">${lbl}</button>`).join('')}</div>
3187  </div>`;
3188}
3189
3190function wireOpsActions(){
3191  document.querySelectorAll('[data-opact]').forEach(btn=>{
3192    btn.onclick=()=>{
3193      const [act,id]=btn.dataset.opact.split('|');
3194      const job=OPS.jobs.find(b=>b.id===id); if(!job) return;
3195      if(act==='detail') { opsCurJob=job; location.hash='#/opsbook'; }
3196      if(act==='confirm') confirmOpsJob(job);
3197      if(act==='collect') collectOpsJob(job);
3198      if(act==='paid')    paidOpsJob(job);
3199      if(act==='invoice') { opsCurJob=job; renderInvPage(job); location.hash='#/opsinv'; }
3200    };
3201  });
3202  document.querySelectorAll('[data-opassign]').forEach(sel=>{
3203    sel.onchange=()=>{
3204      const job=OPS.jobs.find(b=>b.id===sel.dataset.opassign); if(!job) return;
3205      const op=OPS.operators.find(o=>o.id===sel.value); if(!op) return;
3206      assignOpsOperator(job,op);
3207    };
3208  });
3209}

3211async function confirmOpsJob(job){ job.status='assigned'; job.confirmedAt=Date.now(); await saveJob(job); await opsLoad(); renderOps(); }
3212async function collectOpsJob(job){ job.status='collected'; job.collectedAt=Date.now(); await saveJob(job); await opsLoad(); renderOps(); }
3213async function paidOpsJob(job){ job.status='complete'; job.paidAt=Date.now(); await saveJob(job); await opsLoad(); renderOps(); }
3214async function assignOpsOperator(job,op){
3215  Object.assign(job,{operatorId:op.id,operatorName:op.first+' '+op.last,operatorEmail:op.email||'',operatorPhone:op.phone||''});
3216  if(job.status==='requested') job.status='assigned';
3217  job.confirmedAt=Date.now();
3218  await saveJob(job); await opsLoad(); renderOps();
3219}

3241  if(job.status==='invoiced')   acts.push(['btn-ok','paid','✅ Mark paid']);

3243

3246      <div class="ops-b">

3248        <div class="ops-ln"><i>✉️</i><span>${esc(job.email||'—')}</span></div>
3249        <div class="ops-ln"><i>📞</i><span>${esc(job.phone||'—')}</span></div>
3250        <div class="ops-ln"><i>🧺</i><span>${esc(job.service||'')}${job.recurring?' (recurring '+job.recurring+')':''}</span></div>
3251        <div class="ops-ln"><i>📅</i><span>${esc(job.date||'')} · ${esc(job.window||'')}${cal?' <a href="'+cal+'" target="_blank">Open in Calendar</a>':''}</span></div>
3252        <div class="ops-ln"><i>📍</i><span>${esc(job.address||job.suburb||'')}</span></div>
3253        ${job.care?`<div class="ops-ln"><i>👕</i><span>${esc(job.care)}</span></div>`:''}
3254        ${job.delivery?`<div class="ops-ln"><i>🚚</i><span>${esc(job.delivery)}</span></div>`:''}
3255        ${job.operatorName?`<div class="ops-ln"><i>👷</i><span>${esc(job.operatorName)} · ${esc(job.operatorEmail||'')} · ${esc(job.operatorPhone||'')}</span></div>`:''}

3258      </div>
3259    </div>
3260    <div class="ap-card"><div class="ap-card-b">${opSel}<div class="fmsg" id="ojMsg"></div></div></div>

3268    };

3274      if(act==='collect') { collectOpsJob(job); history.back(); }

3302/* ---- invoice page ---- */
3303function renderInvPage(job){
3304  document.getElementById('invBack').onclick=()=>history.back();
3305  document.getElementById('invRef').textContent='Job '+esc(job.ref||job.id);
3306  document.getElementById('invWt').value='';
3307  document.getElementById('invIron').value=job.ironItems||0;
3308  document.getElementById('invNotes').value='';
3309  document.getElementById('invAdj').value='';
3310  document.getElementById('invPreview').style.display='none';
3311  document.getElementById('invCalc').onclick=()=>calcInvoice(job);
3312  document.getElementById('invConfirm').onclick=()=>confirmInvoice(job);
3313}
3314function calcInvoice(job){
3315  const kg=parseFloat(document.getElementById('invWt').value)||0;
3316  const iron=parseInt(document.getElementById('invIron').value)||0;
3317  const adj=parseFloat(document.getElementById('invAdj').value)||0;
3318  const svc=job.svc||'wf';
3319  if(!kg&&svc!=='iron'){ say('invMsg',false,'Enter the actual weight to calculate.'); return; }
3320  let lines=[],sub=0;
3321  if(['wf','wfi'].includes(svc)){
3322    const loads=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(kg/5)), v=loads*PX.load; sub+=v;
3323    lines.push([`${loads} × wash & fold load${loads>1?'s':''} (${kg} kg)`,v]);
3324  } else if(svc==='dc'){
3325    const items=Math.max(1,Math.round(kg/0.5)), v=items*PX.dcDress; sub+=v;
3326    lines.push([`${items} × garment${items>1?'s':''} (dry cleaned)`,v]);
3327  }
3328  if((svc==='wfi'||svc==='iron')&&iron){ const v=iron*PX.iron; sub+=v; lines.push([`${iron} × item${iron>1?'s':''} ironed`,v]); }
3329  lines.push(['Collection & return',PX.delivery]); sub+=PX.delivery;
3330  if(adj){ lines.push([adj>=0?'Adjustment (surcharge)':'Adjustment (discount)',adj]); sub+=adj; }
3331  const total=Math.max(PX.minimum,sub);
3332  if(total>sub+0.001) lines.push(['Minimum order adjustment',total-sub]);
3333  const invNum='INV-'+Date.now().toString(36).slice(-5).toUpperCase();
3334  document.getElementById('invNum').textContent=invNum;
3335  document.getElementById('invLines').innerHTML=lines.map(([l,v])=>`<div class="sumr"><span>${esc(l)}</span><span>${money(v)}</span></div>`).join('');
3336  document.getElementById('invTotAmt').textContent=money(total);
3337  const notes=document.getElementById('invNotes').value.trim();
3338  const body=`Hi ${esc(job.contact||'there')},\n\nYour laundry is ready — here is your invoice.\n\nInvoice: ${invNum}\nBooking: ${job.ref||''}\nService: ${job.service||''}\nDate: ${job.date||''}\n${notes?'Notes: '+notes+'\n':''}\n${lines.map(([l,v])=>l.padEnd(42)+money(v)).join('\n')}\n${'─'.repeat(50)}\nTOTAL DUE: ${money(total)} (GST incl.)\n\nPay online before we can return your laundry:\n[ADD PAYMENT LINK HERE]\n\nVisual, Mastercard, Amex, Google Pay and Apple Pay accepted.\n\nQuestions? Reply to this email.\n\nThank you!\nThe LaundryKings Team`;
3339  document.getElementById('invEmailBox').innerHTML=`<strong>To:</strong> ${esc(job.email||'[email]')}<br><strong>Subject:</strong> LaundryKings — Invoice ${invNum} — ${money(total)}<br><pre>${esc(body)}</pre>`;
3340  document.getElementById('invMailto').href=`mailto:${encodeURIComponent(job.email||'')}?subject=${encodeURIComponent(`LaundryKings — Invoice ${invNum} — ${money(total)}`)}&body=${encodeURIComponent(body)}`;
3341  document.getElementById('invConfirm').dataset.invNum=invNum;
3342  document.getElementById('invConfirm').dataset.total=total;
3343  document.getElementById('invPreview').style.display='';
3344  say('invMsg','','');
3345}

3466function richBookingCard(b){
3467  const d=fromIso(b.date||isoDate(new Date()));
3468  const stMap={requested:'pd',confirmed:'up',assigned:'up',complete:'ok',cancelled:'cx',invoiced:'pd'};
3469  const stLbl={requested:'AWAITING OPERATOR',confirmed:'CONFIRMED',assigned:'CONFIRMED',complete:'COMPLETE',cancelled:'CANCELLED',invoiced:'AWAITING PAYMENT'};
3470  const pill=`<span class="pill ${stMap[b.status]||'pd'}">${stLbl[b.status]||b.status.toUpperCase()}</span>`;
3471  const borderCls=b.status==='cancelled'?'cx':b.status==='complete'?'ok':['assigned','confirmed'].includes(b.status)?'up':'';
3472  const opName=b.operatorName||'Operator TBC';
3473  const acts=[];
3474  if(['requested','assigned'].includes(b.status)) acts.push(`<button class="btn btn-out" data-bkcancel="${b.id}">Cancel</button>`);
3475  if(b.status==='cancelled') acts.push(`<button class="btn btn-out" data-bkrebook="${b.id}">↺ Rebook</button>`);
3476  acts.push(`<button class="btn btn-out" data-bksupport="${b.id}">🎧 Support</button>`);
3477  return `<div class="bkc ${borderCls}">
3478    <div class="bkc-h"><i>🧺</i><strong>${esc(SVCNAME(b.svc))}</strong>${pill}</div>
3479    <div class="bkc-b">
3480      <div class="bkc-r"><i>📅</i><span>${d.toLocaleDateString('en-AU',{weekday:'short',day:'numeric',month:'short'})} · ${esc(b.window||'')}</span></div>
3481      <div class="bkc-r"><i>📍</i><span>${esc(b.address||b.suburb||'')}</span></div>
3482      ${b.recurring?`<div class="bkc-r"><i>🔄</i><span>Repeats ${esc(b.recurring)}</span></div>`:''}
3483      <div class="bkc-footer">
3484        <div class="bkc-op"><div class="bkc-av">LK</div>${esc(opName)}</div>
3485        <span class="opt-p" style="font-size:.82rem">${b.invoiceTotal?money(b.invoiceTotal):b.estimate?'~'+money(b.estimate):'—'}</span>
3486      </div>
3487    </div>
3488    ${acts.length?`<div class="bkc-acts">${acts.join('')}</div>`:''}
3489  </div>`;
3490}

Appendix D

Fact-check register

A second pass, run after the document was built, that re-derived every number from first principles and re-sourced every external fact from at least two independent references where the primary page could not be fetched. Each row states the claim as it appears in the dossier, how it was checked, the result, and what was changed. Rows marked Corrected were fixed in this edition before release.

Method: every page was rendered to an image and inspected; every number was recomputed by script from the tokens, the rate card or the live file; every external fact was searched for a second time with different wording, and accepted only when at least two independent references agreed. Anything that fails that test carries an Unverified or Single-source tag in the body of the dossier, not just here.
